Here is my comprehensive report. --- ## Knowledge Base (知识库) Architecture in prism-agent The codebase has **two coexisting knowledge base systems**: a newer v2 Knowledge Service architecture (SQLite + DataApi + IPC) and a legacy RAG service tied to the Wiki system. Both are used by agents through a unified set of builtin tools. --- ### 1. Core Data Types and Schemas **`C:\Users\chenfei\Documents\GitHub\prism-agent\src\shared\data\types\knowledge.ts`** (823 lines) This is the **single source of truth** for all knowledge domain types. Key concepts: - **KnowledgeBase entity**: stored in SQLite, has `id`, `name`, `embeddingModelId`, `dimensions`, `chunkSize`, `chunkOverlap`, `chunkStrategy`, `threshold`, `rerankModelId`, `fileProcessorId`, `status` (`completed` | `failed`) - **KnowledgeItem entity**: four types -- `file`, `url`, `note`, `directory`. Each has a lifecycle state machine: ``` file/url/note: idle -> processing -> reading -> embedding -> completed directory: idle -> preparing -> processing -> completed ``` - **Two retrieval modes**: Vector base (has embedding model + dimensions) and BM25-only base (no embedding model) - **Search result schema**: `KnowledgeSearchResult` with `pageContent`, `score`, `scoreKind` (`relevance` | `ranking`), `metadata`, `conceptId`, `title` - **Chunk metadata**: `itemId`, `itemType`, `source`, `chunkIndex`, `tokenCount` --- ### 2. 0) > 0` #### b) `kb_search` -- Semantic Search **File**: `C:\Users\chenfei\Documents\GitHub\prism-agent\src\main\ai\tools\adapters\aiSdk\builtin\KnowledgeSearchTool.ts` - Input: `query` (string) + `baseIds` (array of knowledge base IDs) - Returns ranked results with `conceptId`, `title`, `type`, `content`, `score` - `defer: 'always'` - Core logic in `searchKnowledge()` from `knowledgeLookup.ts` #### c) `kb_read` -- Read/Grep Documents **File**: `C:\Users\chenfei\Documents\GitHub\prism-agent\src\main\ai\tools\adapters\aiSdk\builtin\KnowledgeReadTool.ts` - **Two modes**: read full document text (by `conceptId` + `baseId`), or grep with a regex `pattern` - Supports paging via `charStart`/`charEnd` - `defer: 'always'` #### d) `kb_manage` -- Add/Delete/Refresh Documents **File**: `C:\Users\chenfei\Documents\GitHub\prism-agent\src\main\ai\tools\adapters\aiSdk\builtin\KnowledgeManageTool.ts` - Actions: `add` (file/url/note), `delete` (by conceptIds), `refresh` (re-index by conceptIds) - **Approval-gated** (`needsApproval: true`) -- user must approve before execution - `defer: 'never'` (must be inline for approval gate to work) #### Core Lookup Logic **File**: `C:\Users\chenfei\Documents\GitHub\prism-agent\src\main\ai\tools\knowledgeLookup.ts` All four tools delegate to shared functions here: - `searchKnowledge()` -- tries RAG search fir …(+6924)

[-07-30 19:42] TEXT: ## Mini Program 是什么 本质就是一个**内嵌浏览器**，在 Electron webview 里加载外部 AI 网站： ``` 预设列表（50+）: ChatGPT → https://chatgpt.com/ Gemini → https://gemini.google.com/ DeepSeek → https://chat.deepseek.com/ SiliconFlow → https://cloud.siliconflow.cn/playground/chat Kimi → https://kimi.moonshot.cn/ ... ``` 功能就是：点击图标 → 打开一个 tab → webview 加载对应网站 → 可以保活、切换、打印 PDF、保存 HTML。 ## 说实话，必要性确实存疑 **它能做的事：** - 在一个 app 里快速切换多个 AI 网站 - 保活（不重复加载） - 区域过滤（CN/Global） - 打印/保存网页 **但问题是：** - 用户完全可以用浏览器做同样的事，还支持扩展、多窗口 - webview 没有浏览器的隐私隔离、扩展生态 - 后端全烂了（stub + 空 seeder + 无 DB 表），说明这个功能在 v2 迁移中已经被放弃了 - 代码复杂度不低（popup/keep-alive/tab pool/eviction 策略），维护成本高 **结论：** 如果你不需要在 app 内一站式访问多个 AI 网站，这个功能可以考虑直接移除或暂时搁置，把精力放在核心功能上。要移除的话我可以帮你清理相关代码。

[-07-30 20:21] TEXT: Now I have a complete picture. Here is my comprehensive report. --- ## Complete Type/Capability Assignment Logic and the Gap for Non-LLM Models ### Key Finding: There Is No `type` Field The model schema (`ModelConfigSchema` in `packages/provider-registry/src/schemas/model.ts`) has **no `type` field** like `llm`, `tts`, `asr`, etc. Instead, the system uses: - **`capabilities`** array (e.g., `'audio-generation'`, `'audio-transcript'`, `'embedding'`, `'rerank'`, `'image-generation'`, `'reasoning'`, `'function-call'`) - **`inputModalities`** / **`outputModalities`** (e.g., `'text'`, `'audio'`, `'image'`, `'video'`, `'vector'`) - Runtime helper functions like `isTextToSpeechModel()`, `isSpeechToTextModel()`, `isEmbeddingModel()` in `src/shared/utils/model.ts` that check for specific capabilities --- ### How Capabilities Are Assigned During Catalog Generation (`generate-catalog.ts`) The `buildModels()` function at line 200 of `generate-catalog.ts` handles capability assignment in three stages: #### Stage 1: Upstream Metadata Inference (lines 202-205) Models claimed by a creator get their capabilities from the metadata index built by `buildIndex()`. The index merges data from two upstream sources: - **models.dev** (`parseMdEntry` in `upstream.ts`, line 89): Maps `modalities.input/output` to capabilities. `audio` input becomes `'audio-recognition'`; `audio` output becomes `'audio-generation'`. No `'audio-transcript'` is ever inferred. - **OpenRouter** (`parseOrEntry` in `upstream.ts`, line 158): Same modality-to-capability mapping. Also no `'audio-transcript'`. **Critical limitation**: Neither upstream source reports `'audio-transcript'` (the ASR capability). And neither reports TTS/ASR models at all — they focus on LLM/chat models. #### Stage 2: Manual Creator Models Override (lines 207-213) After upstream enrichment, each creator's hand-listed `models` array is merged in. Manual entries always win over upstream data. This is how **ElevenLabs** and **Suno** TTS models get tagged: ```typescript // elevenlabs.ts creator { id: 'elevenlabs-v3', capabilities: ['audio-generation'], // explicitly set inputModalities: ['text'], outputModalities: ['audio'] } ``` #### Stage 3: Post-Processing Tags (lines 215-237) Two post-processing passes tag additional capabilities: 1. **Embedding/Rerank tagging** (lines 216-223): Uses ID pattern matching (`/embed/i`, `/rerank/i`) and the creator's `kind` field. This is the ONLY automated type inference. There is **no equivalent for TTS, ASR, image-generation, or any other non-LLM type**. 2. **Web-search tagging** (lines 226-237): Uses creator-declared `webSearch` prefixes. Explicitly **skips** non-chat models (TTS/ASR) via the modality gate on line 233-234. --- ### How TTS/ASR Models Get Their Capabilities There are **two distinct patterns** in the codebase: #### Pattern A: Creator-Level Models (ElevenLabs, Suno) These creators declare TTS models directly in their `models` array with explicit capabilities. They end up in `models.json` as first-class catalog entries. Example: `elevenlabs-v3` with `capabilities: ['audio-generation']`. #### Pattern B: Provider-Level Overrides (DashScope CosyVoice, Xiaomi MiMo TTS/ASR) These models are **NOT** in `models.json` at all. They exist **only** as entries in `provider-models.json` via provider `overrides`: **DashScope** (`src/providers/dashscope.ts`, lines 55-69): ```typescript { apiModelId: 'cosyvoice-v1', capabilities: { force: ['audio-generation'] }, modelId: 'cosyvoice-v1', …(+5593)

[-07-30 22:52] TEXT: Now I have a complete picture. Here is my detailed report. --- ## Knowledge Base Import Capabilities Report ### 1. Wiki System (LLM-Powered Knowledge Base) **Import Entry Point:** `src/renderer/pages/knowledge/wiki/WikiIngestDialog.tsx` The Wiki system has two ways to add source documents: **A. Manual Text Input (line 36-44)** - User types a source name and pastes text content - Calls `wikiClient.addSource(wikiId, name, content)` which goes through IPC `wiki:source:add` - The `WikiService.addSource()` (at `src/main/services/WikiService.ts`, line 186-211) writes the content to a `raw/` folder on disk and records metadata in SQLite **B. File Upload (line 46-63)** - Uses a standard HTML `<input type="file" multiple>` element - Accepted file types: `.txt,.md,.pdf,.doc,.docx,.html,.htm,.json,.csv,.xml` (line 159) - Reads file content via `file.text()` in the renderer, then sends the raw text to `wikiClient.addSource(wikiId, file.name, text, file.type)` - Files are processed **one at a time in a loop** — no batch optimization **Ingestion Pipeline (WikiAgentService at `src/main/services/WikiAgentService.ts`):** - After sources are added, the user clicks "Ingest" on each source individually - `WikiAgentService.ingest()` reads the source file, the wiki schema, and existing pages, then calls an LLM to extract entities/concepts and create structured wiki pages - The LLM generates JSON with new pages, index updates, and log entries ### 2. RAG System (Vector Embedding Search) **Import Entry Point:** `src/renderer/pages/knowledge/rag/RagDocumentManager.tsx` **File Upload Only (line 33-65):** - Uses a standard HTML `<input type="file" multiple>` element - Accepted file types: `.txt,.md,.pdf,.doc,.docx,.html,.htm,.json,.csv,.xml` (line 118) - Reads file content via `file.text()` in the renderer - Calls `ragClient.ingest(wikiId, file.name, text, file.type)` for each file - Processing is **sequential** — each file is ingested one by one with UI status updates **Ingestion Pipeline (RagService at `src/main/services/RagService.ts`):** - `ingestDocument()` (line 169-272) chunks text (500 chars, 50 overlap), generates embeddings via a configured embedding model, and stores chunks in SQLite - No text input option — file upload only - No URL import, no API import ### 3.
